## Definitions (grade ladder, lowest to highest)

- `candidate` â€” generated/reviewed source exists; not compile-verified (1,066 rows)
- `checker-pass` â€” passes the structural checker only; no compiler involved
- `compiled` â€” builds to a VC7.1 object; no parity claimed (18,737)
- `functional` â€” behavior fixture passes vs local oracle; bytes differ (`DIFFER`) beyond the `near_match` margin â€” counts toward source completeness, not byte totals
- `near_match` â€” genuine C++, behavior fixture passes, and byte-close under VC7.1 (proposed: instruction count Â±10%, â‰¥90% opcode similarity after masking registers/relocs/stack offsets) â€” **counts as reconstructed** (user, 2026-09-26: functionally identical within a reasonable byte margin is the bar); see `docs/modern/MODERNIZATION.md#reconstruction-standard-functional-first-byte-close`
- `matching` â€” `MATCH` (exact `.text`) or `RELOCATION_MATCH` (exact after masking COFF reloc slots) â€” the byte-exact grades; still preferred, not required
- `asm_bake` â€” `__declspec(naked)` / `_emit` byte-bake; byte-exact but NOT genuine C++; purity debt tracked by `crawl/purity.py`
